What new algorithm could be stronger than previous?
- Use **spectral / Fourier amplitude design**: Since c_k = IDFT of P^2, we can choose target coefficients c_k (nonnegative, ≤M, sum=1) and try to factor their DFT as square of a DFT P with inverse nonnegative. This is like designing a polynomial square root with nonnegative coefficients. We can alternate: given desired c, compute P=FFT^{-1} of sqrt(DFT(c))? Need branch and nonneg. Since c=P(z)*P(z) (not autocorr), DFT(c_k)=P(ω_k)^2. If we choose target c_k positive, P(ω)=sqrt(C_k) with phases; need inverse nonnegative. Could use iterative phase? But p nonnegative maybe easier. Let's design **accelerated gradient / Adam in logp**:
```
def adam_logp(p, deadline, tau, lr, beta1, beta2, eps, cmax_target=None):
    x = log(p+eps)
    m = zeros; v=zeros; t=0
    while time<deadline:
        c = fftconv(p)
        cmax = max(c)
        w = softmax((c-cmax)/tau)
        J = cmax + tau logsumexp...
        score=2n*J
        g = grad_from_weights(w) # length n
        # compute grad_x = p*(g - dot(p,g))
        gx = p*(g - np.dot(p,g))
        # Adam update
        m = b1*m+(1-b1)*gx
        v = b2*v+(1-b2)*gx*gx
        x = x - lr * mhat / sqrt(vhat+eps)
        p = softmax(x) (with min/max)
        # maybe line search / trust region: if score worse than best by >tol, revert and reduce lr
    return best
```

Adam implementation details:
```
def optimize_adam(p, deadline, seed, tau0, tau_min, lr0, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.99, quiet):
   p=normalize
   n; N=next_pow2(3n-2)
   x=np.log(p+1e-300); x-=mean; m=0; v=0; t=0
   best_score=internal_score(p,N); best=p.copy()
   lr=lr0; tau=tau0; target=None or maybe current cmax*?
   target_score = best_score*(1-1e-6)?
   stall=0
   while time<deadline:
       c,cmax,smooth,w,g = compute... maybe exact_score=2n*cmax
       gx = p*(g - np.dot(p,g))
       # scale gradient to prevent huge: gx /= max(abs(gx).max(), eps) maybe normalize by n?
       # Adam with lr per step
       t+=1
       m = b1*m+(1-b1)*gx
       v = b2*v+(1-b2)*gx*gx
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
       step = lr * mhat / sqrt(vhat+eps)
       # clip step to max 0.5
       x_try = x - step
       x_try -= np.mean(x_try)
       x_try = np.clip(x_try, -30, 30)
       p_try = softmax_seq(x_try)
       score_try=internal_score(p_try,N)
       # accept if better or smooth improvement? If not, half lr
       if finite and score_try < best_score*(1-1e-12) or score_try < score*(1-1e-9): update
       else: lr=max(1e-5,lr*0.7); if too many, maybe reinitialize lr, change target, reheat
   return best
```

Let's implement `optimize_line_logp`:
```
direction in logp: h = -g + mean(g) (to keep scale). Then p_t = softmax(log p + t h). Evaluate exact score for t grid. Choose best t; update if better; adjust step size.
```
This is gradient line search (single links). More effective than fixed small eta.

```
import time, math, numpy as np
try: import scipy.optimize as sp
except: sp=None

def next_pow2...
def normalize...
def softmax...
def convolve_seq...
def convolve_and_fft...
def apply_A(v,w,N=None):
    n=len(v); if N None next_pow2(3n-2)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(v,N)*fw,N)
    return out[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
def score_and_grad(p, tau, N=None, target_M=None):
   n=len(p); if N None next_pow2(3n-2)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(p,N); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2n-1]
   cmax=max(c)
   if target_M is None or target_M > cmax: base=cmax
   else: base=max(target_M, cmax - 0.04*cmax) #? maybe target
   z=(c-base)/tau; clip; w=exp(z)/sum
   grad=2*apply_A(p,w,N)
   # smooth score if base=cmax: base+tau logsumexp (z by base)
```

```
def optimize_gradient_line(a, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False):
    a = normalize; n; N=next_pow2(3n-2)
    best=...
    loga = np.log(a+1e-300); center
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    eta = 0.10
    beta = 0.08 # width relative to cmax
    base_gap = 0.10 # target below max fraction
    stagnant=0; it=0; last_best=best
    target_ratio = 0.999 # target score relative to best
    while time < deadline:
       c, fa = convolve_and_fft(a,N)
       cmax = max(c); score=2n*cmax
       if update best
       # target M:
       target_score = max(1.01, best_score * 0.9995)  # slightly below best
       target_M = target_score/(2n)
       if target_M > cmax: base = cmax
       else:
          # choose base between target_M and cmax; as score approaches target, target_M ~ cmax*0.9995, base low
          base = max(target_M, cmax - (cmax-target_M))? hmm
       # We want weights emphasize top few. Use z=(c - (cmax - beta*cmax))/tau, with tau=beta? Simpler as previous.
       base = cmax - beta*cmax
       z=(c-base)/(beta*cmax); w=softmax; maybe multiply by target if c<target? no.
       g=grad_from_weights(w, fa,N,n)
       g = g - np.mean(g)
       scale = max(abs(g)); if 0 break; g/=scale
       # Direction can be -g; line search over eta
       # propose a few etas: eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, and sometimes eta*2
       etas = [eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta, min(1.0, 2*eta)] maybe unique sorted desc? We want find improvement.
       best_eta=0; best_score_trial=score; best_smooth=inf
       for e in etas:
           log_try=loga - e*g
           a_try=normalize(exp(clip(log_try)))
           c_try = convolve_seq(a_try,N)
           sc_try=2n*max(c_try)
           # smooth with same base? optional
           accept if sc_try < score*(1-1e-11)
           if accept break? We want maybe largest eta that improves; try descending.
       if accepted: update; eta=min(0.8, eta*1.05); stagnant=0
       else: eta=max(1e-4, eta*0.7); stagnant++
       if stagnant>100:
          # reheat: add random log noise to current, if improves? or reset loga from resampled best with random Fourier noise
          noise = rng.normal(0, 0.1, n); log_try=...; if score_try < best_score: update; or accept temporarily? For escape, we can set a = a * exp(noise) normalize even if score worse? Stochastic hill-climb? Maybe if no improvement, random reheat and keep if not too bad (score < best*1.1). Then reset eta.
```

### Try to reason if full gradient line can beat mirror

Could the last 0.013 be due to integer binary max count one or two? If current length 2048 continuous score 1.516338. Equivalent cmax = score/(2n)=1.516338/4096=0.00036998. If p normalized, target 1.5027 cmax=0.00036687. Difference 0.00000311. If sequence has probabilistic levels, maybe reduces mass by 3e-6. Local enough.

Let's consider evaluating after linear scaling of given sequence? Scale doesn't change. Maybe applying a slight **global transformation** could improve: p_i' = normalize(p_i^γ) or p_i' = normalize(clip(p_i * (1 + a x))). We can sweep simple transformations of the given best:
- power γ ∈ [0.5,2] (raise probabilities) makes distribution more or less concentrated.
- multiply by linear (1+a x) or (1+a x + b x²), with small a,b.
- reverse.
- convolve with small smoothing kernel (e.g., average with neighbor) or sharpen.
Scalings might find a better neighbor at no cost. Implement `scan_transformations(best_a, n)`:
```
for gamma in np.linspace(0.5,1.8,14): p=normalize(p**gamma)
for a in [-0.5,0.5] etc: p=normalize(p*(1+a*x)); require positive floor.
for smoothing r in [0.2,0.5,1,2,5]: p=normalize(gaussian_filter? Use np.convolve with uniform kernel) but kernel convolution changes length; maybe use np.convolve(...,'same')? For probabilities, apply a small 3-point blur: p=(1-β)p + β*(shift avg) boundary.
```
These are cheap and can cross local minima. We'll evaluate all for several n.

Also combine with another seed: p=(1-mix)a+mix b for mix values. Use best from given and profiles. Could improve.

Implement `apply_simple_transforms(a, n_time)` maybe with time budget. It returns candidate if better. Since objective nonlinear, random transforms can help.

### Let's design code step-by-step
Top-level functions no closures. Need no lambda. Use try except imports.

Functions:
1. `next_pow2(m)`
2. `normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0)`
3. `softmax_seq(z)`
4. `make_uniform(n)`

7. `make_exponential(n, slope)`
8. `make_sine(n, coeffs)` maybe coefficients list (amp, freq, phase)

10. `get_given_sequence()`
11. `resample_seq(a, n)`

16. `smooth_score_from_c(c, base, tau)`
17. `score_grad_smooth(a, tau, base, N=None)` returns exact, smooth, cmax, c, w, g. Maybe use `fa` for grad.
18. `optimize_gradient_line(a, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False)`

Need be mindful of memory and time. We can use global `START_TIME`? Inside propose.

Let's implement details.

#### normalize_positive
If floor=0, `np.clip(a, floor, None)`. For very small values, sum maybe zero. Return float64.

#### softmax_seq
Clip log to [-30,0] to avoid underflow; max 1e-15 denominator.

#### convolve_seq
Use N next_pow2(2n-1). `fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2n-1]`. Due numerical, c coefficients could be tiny negative? Nonnegative but use `np.maximum(c,0)`? For score max, negatives not matter; for max positive. For binary exact, after FFT round, may have ±1e-6. Use rint.

#### convolve_and_fft
N=next_pow2(3n-2) to fit grad. Compute fa, c. Return c, fa.

#### grad_from_weights
```
def grad_from_weights(w, fa, N, n):
    # w length 2n-1
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N)
    return 2.0 * out[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Need ensure w length ≤N. If `w` maybe length 2n-1. Good.

#### score_grad_smooth
```
def score_grad_smooth(a, tau, base, N=None):
    a=np.asarray; n=a.size
    if N is None: N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    c, fa = convolve_and_fft(a,N)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <=0 or not finite: return inf,0,0,c,np.full(L,1/L),zeros
    smooth = smooth_score_from_c(c, base, tau)
    z=(c-base)/tau; z=np.clip(z,-50,50?) if base < max, z positive up to (cmax-base)/tau. If base=cmax, max 0; if base lower, positive. For exp overflow, actual softmax should exp(z - maxz) to be stable. We can do:
       z = (c-base)/tau
       zmax=max(z); ez=np.exp(z-zmax); s=sum; w=ez/s
    grad=grad_from_weights(w, fa, N, n)
    exact=2n*cmax
    return exact, smooth, cmax, c, w, grad
```
But gradient w should be normalized probabilities; using exp shifted same. If tau very small and zmax huge, okay. Bonus: gradient w for smooth value with base logsumexp? The smooth value is base + tau log(Σ exp((c-base)/tau)). Its derivative uses w=exp((c-base)/tau)/Σ; same. Good.

`smooth_score_from_c`: if τ<=0 return base? If base=cmax? We'll handle.
```
z=(c-base)/tau; zmax=max(z); return base+tau*(zmax+log(sum(exp(z-zmax))))
```
If base != cmax, smooth_value not exactly max smoothing? It's logsumexp relative base. Fine.

#### optimize_gradient_line
Detailed implementation:
```
def optimize_gradient_line(a, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False):
    a=normalize_positive(a, 1e-15)
    n=a.size; N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score(a,N)
    if not finite or best_score<=1.0005: return
    loga=np.log(a+1e-300); loga-=np.mean(loga)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    eta=0.05
    tau_rel=0.12
    focus_rel=0.12 # base = cmax - focus*cmax (top 10% maybe)
    target_score=None # maybe pass? use global best? We can use current best_score itself
    stagnant=0; it=0; last_best=best_score
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c, fa = convolve_and_fft(a,N)
       cmax=float(np.max(c)); score=2*n*cmax
       if finite and score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=best_score
       if cmax <=0: break
       # base and tau:
       # If score close to target lower, make focus smaller? focus = max(0.02, min(0.2, 0.3*(score-1.0)/0.5?)). Simpler: schedule based on stagnant? 
       focus = focus_rel
       tau = max(1e-12, tau_rel*cmax)
       base = cmax - focus*cmax
       # If score >2? maybe focus small? 
       exact, smooth, cmax, c, w, g = score_grad_smooth(a, tau, base, N)
       if not finite smooth: break
       g = g - float(np.mean(g))
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if scale<=1e-15:
           # try random perturb
       g = g/scale
       # candidate etas descending: start eta, then larger? Use [eta, 0.5eta, 0.25eta, 2eta]
       etas=[]
       for f in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0, 4.0? maybe too big):
          val=eta*f
          if val<1e-6: continue
          if val>2.0: val=2.0
          if all(abs(val-ex)>1e-9 for ex in etas): etas.append(val)
       # sort descending? Try largest that improves; but too large may bad. Use descending from min(1,eta*2), eta, half, quarter.
       etas.sort(reverse=True)
       accepted=False
       for e in etas:
          log_try=loga - e*g
          log_try-=np.mean(log_try)
          a_try=normalize_positive(np.exp(np.clip(log_try,-40,0)),1e-15)
          c_try=convolve_seq(a_try, N) # can use exact; N large enough
          sc_try=2*n*float(np.max(c_try))
          # smooth try with same base tau:
          if np.isfinite(sc_try):
             smooth_try=smooth_score_from_c(c_try, base, tau)
             if (sc_try < score*(1-1e-11)) or (smooth_try < smooth*(1-1e-8) and sc_try < score*1.01):
                 a=a_try; loga=...
                accepted=True
                 if sc_try < best_score: best_score=sc_try; best_a=a.copy()
                 score=sc_try
                 break
       if accepted:
           stagnant=0; eta=min(0.5, eta*1.03)
           # slowly reduce focus/tau? 
           tau_rel=max(0.03, tau_rel*0.999)
           focus_rel=max(0.02, focus_rel*0.998)
       else:
           stagnant +=1; eta=max(1e-4, eta*0.7)
           if stagnant % 200 ==0:
               tau_rel=min(0.3, tau_rel*1.5)
               focus_rel=min(0.3, focus_rel*1.5)
           if stagnant > 800 or (it>0 and it%1000==0 and best_score > last_best - 1e-9):
               # reheat: random log noise with low-pass
               noise = rng.normal(0, 0.15, n)
               # smooth noise with few modes? could be high freq; use moving average cumulative? Simpler: low-pass by cumulative integral? Use forward cumulative and subtract.
               # For escape, use random Fourier perturbation:
               ptry = a * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -2,2)); normalize; if score < best_score*1.1 accept maybe
               # choose best among several random perturbations
               candidate=None
               for amp in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
                  nz=rng.normal(0,1,n)
                  # low-pass: cumulative average? Use np.convolve with small kernel if length.
                  ker=np.ones(max(1,n//200)); nz=np.convolve(nz, ker, 'same'); 
                  nz/=max(np.linalg.norm(nz),1e-12)
                  p2=normalize_positive(a*np.exp(amp*nz),1e-15)
                  sc2=internal_score(p2,N)
                  if finite and (c2 < score? or best): choose
               if candidate is not None and score_candidate < best_score:
                  a=p2; loga=...
                  stagnant=0
               elif candidate is not None:
                  # accept even if not better but not terrible for exploration? Maybe not, mirror descent needs gradient.
       it +=1
       if best_score <=1.001: break
       if verbose and it%100==0: print
    return best_a,best_score
```
Need avoid high-freq noise causing score huge. Use sine perturbations:
```
def random_log_perturb(a, amp, rng, n):
   k1=1+rng.integers(0,5); phase...
   mode=sum amp/modes * sin(2π m x+φ)
```
Will write helper `random_log_noise(n, amp, seed)` using a few low modes.
Maybe simpler: use random uniformly with sizes for a few Fourier modes 1-8; sum; normalize noise. This is smooth and likely safe.
```
def random_smooth_noise(n, amp, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=zeros
    for m in range(1,6):
       f=1+rng.integers(0, max(1,int(n/64)))
       out += rng.normal(0,1)/np.sqrt(m)*np.sin(2π f x/r? Wait f cycles)
    out *= amp / (np.linalg.norm(out)+eps)
    return out
```
Better: apply to log directly.

In reheat, choose candidate with lower score; if even best only, update a to best_a and loga = log(best_a) + amp*noise? To escape local, starting from best plus noise is good. We can set loga = log(best_a)+ noise; a=softmax. We don't need accept worse. It will explore near best.

But optimize_gradient_line starting from best may get stuck; reheat with noise can find side.

#### optimize_binary
Detailed robust:

```
def make_binary_from_values(a, m): ...
def optimize_binary(a, deadline, seed=0, m=None, target_score=None):
   a=norm
   n=a.size
   if m is None: m=int(round(0.5*n))
   b = threshold top m
   Nc=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   c = np.rint(convolve_seq(b, Nc)).astype(float)
   # ensure c sum m^2? c sum = m^2. Could adjust: if c[0] not b0²? no.
   best_b=b.copy(); best_max=max(c); best_score=2n*best_max/(m*m)
   rng
   temp=0.05
   beta=0.15
   stale=0
   two_b=2.0*b
   iterations=0
   # If target_score: target_max = target_score*m*m/(2n); maybe use floor
   target_max = (target_score if target_score else 2.0)*m*m/(2*n)
   while time < deadline:
       cmax=float(np.max(c)); score=2n*cmax/(m*m)
       if score < best_score: update
       if cmax <= max(1.0, target_max*0.999): maybe continue? Could break if no lower? But counts integer; if cmax == floor(target_max) maybe optimum? Not.
       # weights for pressure
       # Use w proportional to exp((c - (cmax - beta*cmax))/ (beta*cmax))
       base=cmax - beta*cmax
       z=(c-base)/(beta*cmax); w=softmax
       # if target_max < cmax, also weight above target:
       if target_max < cmax:
          base2=max(target_max, cmax*0.9)
          z2=(c-base2)/(max(1.0, target_max*0.1)); w2=softmax
          w=0.5*w+0.5*w2
       fa=np.fft.rfft(b,N) with N=next_pow2(3n-2) maybe; g=grad_from_weights(w, fa, next_pow2(3n-2)? mismatch c? Need N consistent with w length. We can compute Ngrad=next_pow2(3n-2); fa=rfft(b,Ngrad); g=grad...
       # choose one high, zero low
       one_idx=np.nonzero(b>0.5)[0]; zero_idx=...
       if empty break
       # Maybe choose one with pressure g among ones high, zero low.
       # For binary, removing a 1 should reduce high lags: high g likely; adding to low g.
       si = softmax_select(g[one_idx], high=True, temp=0.2)
       sj = softmax_select(g[zero_idx], high=False, temp=0.2)
       i=one_idx[si]; j=zero_idx[sj]
       # update
       ci=c # current
       c[i:i+n] -= two_b
       c[2*i] -= 1
       c[j:j+n] += two_b
       c[2*j] += 1
       b[i]=0; b[j]=1; two_b=2*b
       new_max=float(np.max(c))
       new_score=2n*new_max/(m*m)
       accept=False
       if new_score < score*(1-1e-12): accept=True
       elif new_score == score: # tie: compare sum of top 16? cheap maybe
          if np.sum(c[np.argsort(c)[-16:]]) < previous_top_sum? Need prev. Could ignore.
          if rng.random()<0.2: accept=True
       else:
          delta=new_score-score
          if delta < 0.02: accept=rng.random() < exp(-delta/temp)
       if accept:
          if new_score<best_score update
          stale=0; temp=max(0.001,temp*0.999)
       else:
          # revert using current b (which already swapped)
          c[j:j+n] -= two_b
          c[2*j] -=1
          c[i:i+n] += two_b
          c[2*i] +=1
          b[j]=0; b[i]=1; two_b=2*b
          stale+=1; temp=max(0.0005,temp*0.97)
          if stale%200==0: temp=min(0.15,temp*1.6); beta=min(0.4,beta*1.4); stale=0
          if stale>1000: break
       iterations+=1
   return normalize_positive(best_b,0), best_score
```
Need `choose_by_pressure` function top-level:
```
def choose_by_pressure(vals, rng, pick_high, temp=0.15):
  m=len; if m==0 return -1
  if m==1 return 0
  vmin=min; vmax=max; denom=max(temp,1e-12)*(vmax-vmin)
  if range<=1e-14: rng int
  if pick_high: z=(vals-vmax)/denom else z=(vmin-vals)/denom
  z=clip(z,-40,0); p=exp(z)/sum; return rng.choice
```
Need ensure c values remain near integers. Floating errors can accumulate over many swaps; after e.g. 5000 accepted swaps, c can drift. We can renormalize:

Maybe use **greedy balance start**: Start with empty set, add positions that currently have lowest count after addition. This can produce balanced sum count at density fraction. Let's see if implementable efficiently for n=2048/4096 without too slow.
We can update counts c for adding positions. To choose next position greedily, for current b, adding position j changes c by 2*shift b + e at 2j. The new max for j = max(c, 2*b shifted? +...). We need a scalar score for j. We can precompute a pressure P(j)=max over lags after addition? Too expensive. But we can approximate by P(j)=max_k (c_k + 2 b_{|?|}+...) across k? Equivalent `new_c_max(j) = max( c, c + shift(b, j) + shift(b,? ) + 1)`. We can compute an array `add_pressure[j]` of the maximum increase over all lags due to adding j: increase to c at lag k is 2 b_{k-j or j-k?} + ... The max over k is 2*max_t? Actually adding one 1 at j increases c_k by b_{k-j}+b_{j-k}? +1 at 2j. The maximum k of this addition is at k where b shifted sum maximal. Since b is binary density, max addition ~2 (if b at both?) +1. Constant. Greedy should reduce local by choosing j that increases lags currently low. We can compute for each j the vector delta_j = 2*(b shifted left? and right?)? This is O(n²). Not.

Use **randomized greedy via gradient**: Start with random binary; repeatedly swap to reduce max. SA does this.

Maybe binary search from best continuous threshold is best.

#### Simple transformation sweeps
Implement in main after initial profiles:
```
def try_transform_sequence(a, deadline, verbose=False):
   best_a=a; best=internal_score
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # gamma powers
   for gamma in [0.5,0.7,0.8,0.9,1.1,1.25,1.5,2.0]:
      p=normalize_positive(np.power(a, gamma))
      update
   # linear multipliers
   for c0 in [-0.9,-0.5,-0.2,0,0.2,0.5,0.9]:
      for c1 in [-0.5,0,0.5]:
         p=normalize_positive(a*(1+c0*x+c1*x*x), floor=1e-12)
   # reverse
   p=a[::-1]
   # gaussian blur/sharp
   for beta in [0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0,2.0,5.0]:
      smoothed = beta*a + (a + shift)/2? Define shift boundaries: left = [a[0], a[:-1]], right = [a[1:], a[-1]]
      p=normalize((1-beta)*a + beta*(left+right)/2) but if beta >1? Use uniform kernel length.
   # local random perturbations (10)
   return best
```
Evaluate many transforms O(num * FFT). For n=8192, 80*2ms=160ms. Fine.

Need avoid `np.power(a, gamma)` where a has zeros; zero**negative? gamma positive. fine. If gamma<1, zeros stay zero, may reduce support; objective? Could be okay, but if many zeros endpoint support smaller score maybe larger. floor 1e-15 before power? If floor small, power may create tiny values. Use a+1e-300 for log? For gamma <1, zeros ->0; that's allowed. But if sum becomes 0? no.

Transformation sweeps can discover better from given. If given not present, still.

### Testing mental for bugs
- In `score_grad_smooth`, if `base` > max(c)? We set base≤cmax. If cmax-zero? handle.
- `tau` could be too small causing zmax large; shift handles.
- `grad_from_weights` uses `w_rev` length L; if N padded, rfft of w_rev with N; okay. But `w_rev` last elements? If N > L, zeros.
- `convolve_seq` with N from caller: For exact score in optimize, we use N=3n-2 maybe which is ≥2n-1; ok.
- For binary gradient, N=3n-2; c exact from N=2n-1; weights w length 2n-1. `grad_from_weights` with N=3n-2 returns length n. fine.

`try_transform(a, deadline)`: Use time check inside. Need if a length n; evaluate with N from internal_score (choose 2n pow2). Many transforms. It should return best found and score.
```
def try_transform(a, deadline, seed=1):
   a=normalize; n=len; best=internal_score
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # gamma
   for gamma in (0.5,0.7,0.8,0.9,1.1,1.3,1.6,2.0):
       p=normalize_positive(np.maximum(a,1e-300)**gamma, 0.0)
       update
   # multipliers
   for c0 in (-1.2,-0.8,-0.5,-0.2,0,0.2,0.5,0.8,1.2):
      for c1 in (-1.0,-0.5,0,0.5,1.0):
         mult=1+c0*x+c1*x*x
         p=normalize_positive(a*mult,1e-14)
         update
      if time> deadline: break
   # blur
   for beta in (0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
      left = np.empty(n); left[0]=a[0]; left[1:]=a[:-1]
      right = np.empty(n); right[:-1]=a[1:]; right[-1]=a[-1]
      if beta<1: p=(1-beta)*a+0.5*beta*(left+right)
      else: # uniform kernel size int(beta)+1
         k=1+int(round(beta))
         ker=np.ones(k)/k
         p=np.convolve(a, ker, 'same')
         p=normalize_positive(p,1e-14)
   # reverse
   p=a[::-1]
   # random smooth perturbations
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   for amp in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5):
      nz=random_smooth_noise(n, amp, rng)
      p=normalize_positive(a*np.exp(nz),1e-14)
   return best
```
For beta=1.0, uniform kernel length 2 gives average; okay. For beta=0.1, 90% original.
Need avoid c1 negative leading all mult≤0; normalize floor creates zeros? If mult has zeros and a*mult zero, sum maybe from others. If all zero, normalize returns ones; internal score 2. Fine.

Function:
```
def apply_swap(b, c, two_b, i, j, n):
    # i has 1, j has 0
    c[i:i+n] -= two_b
    c[2*i] += 1.0
    b[i]=0.0
    two_b[i] = 0.0
    c[j:j+n] += two_b
    c[2*j] += 1.0
    b[j]=1.0
    two_b[j] = 2.0
```

Detailed code:
```
import time
import numpy as np

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
`(int(m)-1).bit_length()` okay.

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a, floor=0.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.clip(a, float(floor), None)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/float(a.size)
    return a/s
```
Floor maybe 0.0; zeros okay.

`softmax_seq`:
```
def softmax_seq(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z=z-np.max(z)
    z=np.clip(z,-60,0)
    e=np.exp(z); e=np.maximum(e,1e-300)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return np.full(z.size,1/z.size)
    return e/s
```

`make_power` etc.

`random_smooth_noise`:
```
def random_smooth_noise(n, amp, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    out=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1,7):
        f = 0.25*m + rng.uniform(0,1.5)
        ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        out += np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)/np.sqrt(m)
        out2 = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        out += np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + out2)/np.sqrt(m)
    norm = np.linalg.norm(out)
    if norm > 1e-12:
        out *= float(amp)/norm
    return out
```
This uses noninteger frequencies; okay.

`convolve_seq`:
```
def convolve_seq(a, N=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel(); n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
   if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   need=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   if N < need: N=need
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
```
`convolve_and_fft`: N=next_pow2(3n-2) for grad; but if N less than 2n-1 adjust. Return c, fa.

`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(a, N=None):
   a=normalize_positive(a,0.0); n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.inf
   c=convolve_seq(a,N)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15: return np.inf
   exact=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)
   # if evaluating after normalize sum=1, but normalize did sum 1. Use general.
   if not np.isfinite(exact): return np.inf
   return float(exact)
```
But if a normalized, s=1. If caller passes b integer, internal_score will normalize and compute 2n*max(c_norm)/1 =2n*max(c)/m². Good. So we can use internal_score for binary too but costly. For binary we compute directly.

`smooth_score_from_c`:
```
def smooth_score_from_c(c, base, tau):
   L=c.size
   if L==0: return 0.0
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if tau <= 1e-300: return float(base) if base>0 else cmax
   z=(c-base)/tau
   zmax=float(np.max(z))
   ez=np.exp(z-zmax)
   s=float(ez.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return float(cmax)
   return float(base + tau*(zmax+np.log(s)))
```
If base maybe >cmax? not.

`make_binary_from_values`:
```
def make_binary_from_values(a, m):
   a=np.asarray(a).ravel(); n=a.size
   m=int(max(0,min(n,m)))
   if m<=0: return zeros
   if m>=n: return ones
   # if already binary counts exactly m, threshold? We can still sort values.
   order=np.argsort(a, kind='stable')
   b=np.zeros(n)
   b[order[-m:]]=1.0
   return b
```
If a has ties, stable.

`apply_binary_swap` as above.
```
def apply_binary_swap(b,c,two_b,i,j,n):
    c[i:i+n] -= two_b
    c[2*i] += 1.0
    b[i]=0.0
    two_b[i]=0.0
    c[j:j+n] += two_b
    c[2*j] += 1.0
    b[j]=1.0
    two_b[j]=2.0
```

`optimize_binary`:

```
def optimize_binary(a, deadline, seed=0, m=None, target_score=None):
   a=np.asarray(a); n=a.size
   if n <8: return normalize(a), internal_score(a)
   if m is None: m=int(round(0.5*n))
   m=max(2,min(n-1,m))
   b=make_binary_from_values(a,m)
   Nc=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   c=np.rint(convolve_seq(b, Nc)).astype(float)
   two_b=2.0*b
   best_b=b.copy(); best_max=float(np.max(c)); best_score=2*n*best_max/(m*m)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   temp=0.05; beta=0.12; stale=0; it=0
   target_max = None
   if target_score is not None:
       target_max = max(1.0, target_score*m*m/(2.0*n))
   # precompute Ngrad
   Ngrad=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      cmax=float(np.max(c)); score=2*n*cmax/(m*m)
      if score < best_score*(1-1e-12): update
      if cmax <= max(0.0, (target_max if target_max else 0.0)+0.25) and target_max is not None:
          # if at or below target integer, might still improve? we can break if cmax <= floor(target_max) and no stochastic? 
          # Continue but with small temp.
      # weights
      if target_max is not None and cmax > target_max:
          base2=max(1.0, target_max*0.9)
          z2=(c-base2)/max(1.0, base2*0.1)
          # only c>base2 get weight; clip
          z2=np.clip(z2,-40,50)
          ez=np.exp(z2-np.max(z2)); w=ez/sum
      else:
          base=cmax - beta*cmax
          z=(c-base)/max(1e-12, beta*cmax)
          ... w
      # gradient pressure
      fa=np.fft.rfft(b,Ngrad)
      g=grad_from_weights(w, fa, Ngrad, n)
      one_idx=np.nonzero(b>0.5)[0]; zero_idx=np.nonzero(b<0.5)[0]
      if not sizes: break
      si=choose_by_pressure(g[one_idx], rng, True, temp=0.15)
      sj=choose_by_pressure(g[zero_idx], rng, False, temp=0.15)
      if si<0 or sj<0: break
      i=int(one_idx[si]); j=int(zero_idx[sj])
      old_score=score
      apply_binary_swap(b,c,two_b,i,j,n)
      new_max=float(np.max(c)); new_score=2*n*new_max/(m*m)
      accept=False
      if new_score < old_score*(1-1e-12): accept=True
      elif new_score <= old_score*(1+1e-12):
          if rng.random() < 0.25: accept=True
      else:
          delta=new_score-old_score
          if delta < 0.02 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/temp): accept=True
      if accept:
         if new_score < best_score: best update
         stale=0; temp=max(0.001,temp*0.999)
      else:
         apply_binary_swap(b,c,two_b,j,i,n)
         stale+=1; temp=max(0.0005,temp*0.97)
         if stale%150==0: temp=min(0.12,temp*1.8); beta=min(0.4,beta*1.5); stale=0
         if stale>1200: break
      it+=1
      if it % 2000 ==0:
         # renormalize c
         c=np.rint(c)
         if abs(float(c.sum()) - float(m*m)) > max(10.0, 1e-3*m*m):
            c=np.rint(convolve_seq(b, Nc)).astype(float)
   return normalize_positive(best_b,0.0), best_score
```

```
def optimize_gradient_line(a, deadline, seed=0, verbose=False):
   a=normalize_positive(a,1e-15); n=a.size
   if n<8: return a, internal_score(a)
   N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score(a,N)  # internal uses convolve_seq maybe N? It can take N but expects 2n; passing N bigger ok.
   if not finite or best_score<=1.0000001: return
   loga=np.log(a+1e-300); loga-=np.mean(loga)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   eta=0.08; tau_rel=0.12; focus_rel=0.12
   stagnant=0; it=0; last_best=best_score
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c, fa = convolve_and_fft(a,N)
       cmax=float(np.max(c)); score=2*n*cmax # a sum1
       if finite and score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=best_score
       if cmax <=1e-300: break
       focus = max(0.02, min(0.35, focus_rel))
       tau = max(1e-12, tau_rel*cmax)
       base = cmax - focus*cmax
       if base < 0: base=0.0
       exact, smooth, cmax, c, w, g = score_grad_smooth(a, tau, base, N)
       if not finite(smooth or exact): break? 
       g = g - np.mean(g)
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if scale <=1e-15:
           stagnant +=1
       else:
           g = g/scale
           eta_candidates=[]
           for mult in (2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25):
              val=eta*mult
              if val <1e-6: continue
              val=min(val,1.5)
              dup=False
              for ex in eta_candidates:
                 if abs(val-ex)<1e-4: dup=True
              if not dup: eta_candidates.append(val)
           eta_candidates.sort(reverse=True)
           accepted=False
           for e in eta_candidates:
              log_try=loga - e*g
              log_try -= np.mean(log_try)
              log_try = np.clip(log_try, -40.0, 0.0)
              a_try = np.exp(log_try); a_try/=sum
              if a_try.sum()<=...: continue
              a_try=normalize_positive(a_try,1e-15)
              c_try=np.fft.irfft(fa? no p changed, need new fa) We have convolve_seq.
              sc_try=2*n*float(np.max(convolve_seq(a_try,N)))
              if finite:
                  smooth_try=smooth_score_from_c(c_try, base, tau)
                  if sc_try < score*(1-1e-11):
                      accept
                  elif smooth_try < smooth*(1-1e-7) and sc_try < score*1.02:
                      accept
              if accept: update a, loga; score=sc_try; break
           if accepted:
              stagnant=0; eta=min(0.6, eta*1.04)
              tau_rel=max(0.03, tau_rel*0.999)
              focus_rel=max(0.02, focus_rel*0.998)
           else:
              stagnant+=1; eta=max(1e-4, eta*0.72)
       if stagnant > 700:
           # reheat from best plus random noise
           best_p=best_a
           improved_candidate=None; best_cand_score=best_score
           for amp in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6):
               nz=random_smooth_noise(n, amp, rng)
               log_try=np.log(best_p+1e-300)+nz
               a_try=normalize_positive(np.exp(np.clip(log_try-np.max(log_try),-40,0)),1e-15)
               sc_try=internal_score(a_try,N)
               if finite and sc_try < best_cand_score: ...
           if improved_candidate is not None:
              a=improved_candidate; loga=log(a); best_score candidate?
           else:
              # move to best and reset
              a=best_a.copy(); loga=np.log(a+...)-mean
           stagnant=0; eta=0.08; tau_rel=0.15; focus_rel=0.15
           if score > best_score*1.1: a=best_a; score=best_score
       it+=1
       if verbose and it % 250==0: print
       if best_score <=1.001: break
   return best_a,best_score
```

```
      gx=a*(g - np.dot(a,g)); scale=max; if scale... break; gx/=scale
      t+=1; ... step
      x_try=x-step; center; clip; a_try=softmax
      c_try=...; sc_try=...
      smooth_try from c_try same base tau
      if accept: x=x_try; a=a_try; update; lr=min(0.1,lr*1.03); stagnant=0
      else: lr=max(1e-4,lr*0.75); stagnant++
         if lr<1e-5 or stagnant>800: reheat similar; break? 
   return best
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2

with a hybrid stochastic/gradient method:
  - profile and transformation sweeps,
  - smooth max subgradient line-search in log-space,
  - Adam-like log-space polishing,
  - integer binary cost-array local search with correct swap bookkeeping.

The best sequence found before the deadline is returned.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a, floor: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, float(floor), None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def softmax_seq(z) -> np.ndarray:
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    e = np.maximum(e, 1e-300)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def make_uniform(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    n = max(1, int(n))
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def make_power(n: int, alpha: float, left: bool = True) -> np.ndarray:
    n = max(2, int(n))
    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if left:
        a = i ** (-float(alpha))
    else:
        a = (i[::-1]) ** (-float(alpha))
    return normalize_positive(a, 1e-15)


def make_exponential(n: int, slope: float) -> np.ndarray:
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_positive(np.exp(float(slope) * x), 1e-15)


def make_gaussian(n: int, mu: float, sigma: float) -> np.ndarray:
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    sigma = max(1e-3, float(sigma))
    return normalize_positive(np.exp(-((x - float(mu)) ** 2) / (2.0 * sigma * sigma)), 1e-15)


def make_fourier_random(n: int, seed: int, max_modes: int = 7) -> np.ndarray:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, max(2, int(max_modes))):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.38) / float(m)
        f = 0.25 * float(m) + rng.uniform(0.0, 1.75)
        ph1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        a += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph1)
        a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph2)
    return normalize_positive(a, 1e-12)


def generate_initial_sequences(n: int, seed: int, max_count: int = 40):
    n = max(16, int(n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    seqs = []
    seqs.append(make_uniform(n))

    for alpha in (0.35, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.70, 0.85, 1.05):
        seqs.append(make_power(n, alpha, True))
        seqs.append(make_power(n, alpha, False))

    for slope in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.2, -0.6, 0.6, 1.2, 2.0, 3.0):
        seqs.append(make_exponential(n, slope))

    for mu in (0.25, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65, 0.75):
        for sigma in (0.09, 0.16, 0.28):
            seqs.append(make_gaussian(n, mu, sigma))

    for k in range(12):
        seqs.append(make_fourier_random(n, int(seed) + 100 + k, max_modes=run parameters
                                         if False else 7))

    # A few deterministic linear shapes.
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    for c0 in (-0.8, -0.4, -0.2, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8):
        for c1 in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8):
            seqs.append(normalize_positive(1.0 + c0 * x + c1 * x * x, 1e-12))

    if max_count is not None and len(seqs) > int(max_count):
        keep = list(range(min(8, len(seqs))))
        rest = list(range(len(keep), len(seqs)))
        rng.shuffle(rest)
        keep.extend(rest[: int(max_count) - len(keep)])
        seqs = [seqs[i] for i in keep]
    return seqs


def resample_seq(a, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    a = normalize_positive(a, 0.0)
    n = max(2, int(n))
    old_n = int(a.size)
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == n:
        return a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return normalize_positive(b, 1e-12)


def get_given_sequence():
    try:
        seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if seq is None:
            return None
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return None
        a = normalize_positive(a, 0.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return None
        return a
    except Exception:
        return None


def convolve_seq(a, N=None) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    need = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    if N is None:
        N = need
    elif int(N) < need:
        N = need
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, int(N))
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, int(N))[: 2 * n - 1]


def convolve_and_fft(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    need_conv = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    need_grad = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    if N is None:
        N = max(need_conv, need_grad)
    else:
        N = max(int(N), need_conv, need_grad)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, int(N))
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, int(N))[: 2 * n - 1]
    return c, fa, int(N)


def grad_from_weights(w, fa, N: int, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, int(N))
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, int(N))
    return 2.0 * out[n - 1: 2 * n - 1][::-1]


def internal_score(a, N=None) -> float:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = convolve_seq(a, N)
    v = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return float("inf")
    return float(v)


def smooth_score_from_c(c, base, tau) -> float:
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return 0.0
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return float(base) if np.isfinite(base) and base >= 0.0 else 0.0
    if tau <= 1e-300:
        return float(cmax)
    z = (c - float(base)) / float(tau)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float(cmax)
    return float(float(base) + float(tau) * (zmax + float(np.log(s))))


def score_grad_smooth(a, tau, base, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    c, fa, N = convolve_and_fft(a, N)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf"), 0.0, 0.0, c, np.ones(c.size) / max(1, c.size), np.zeros(n)
    a = a / s
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    exact = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
    L = int(c.size)
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return float(exact), float(base), cmax, c, w, g

    if base is None or not np.isfinite(base) or base > cmax:
        base = cmax
    base = float(base)
    tau = max(1e-12, float(tau))
    z = (c - base) / tau
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    ss = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = ez / ss
    g = grad_from_weights(w, fa, N, n)
    smooth = float(base + tau * (zmax + float(np.log(ss))))
    return float(exact), float(smooth), cmax, c, w, g


def random_smooth_noise(n: int, amp: float, rng) -> np.ndarray:
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 7):
        f = 0.25 * float(m) + rng.uniform(0.0, 1.75)
        p1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        p2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        out += (np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + p1) + np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + p2)) / np.sqrt(float(m))
    nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(out))
    if nrm > 1e-14:
        out *= float(amp) / nrm
    return out


def try_transforms(a, deadline) -> tuple:
    a = normalize_positive(a, 1e-15)
    n = int(a.size)
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = internal_score(a, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or n < 2:
        return best_a, best_score

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345+7)

    def consider(p):
        nonlocal best_a, best_score
        p = normalize_positive(p, 1e-15)
        sc = internal_score(p, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = p.copy()
            print("transform score = {:.9f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() > deadline:
        return best_a, best_score

    # Power scaling.
    for gamma in (0.55, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 1.1, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0):
        consider(np.maximum(a, 1e-300) ** float(gamma))
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    # Low-order polynomial multipliers.
    for c0 in (-1.3, -0.9, -0.6, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.3):
        for c1 in (-1.2, -0.7, -0.35, 0.0, 0.35, 0.7, 1.2):
            consider(a * (1.0 + c0 * x + c1 * x * x))
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    # Reversal.
    consider(a[::-1])

    # Blurs / sharpeners.
    if n > 1:
        left = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        right = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        left[0] = a[0]
        left[1:] = a[:-1]
        right[-1] = a[-1]
        right[:-1] = a[1:]

        for beta in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.80, 1.20, 1.80, 3.00):
            if beta <= 1.0:
                consider((1.0 - beta) * a + beta * 0.5 * (left + right))
            else:
                k = 1 + int(round(beta))
                k = min(max(3, k), max(3, n // 2))
                if k % 2 == 0:
                    k += 1
                ker = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
                consider(np.convolve(a, ker, mode="same"))
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            return best_a, best_score

    # Smooth random log perturbations.
    for amp in (0.04, 0.08, 0.15, 0.25, 0.40, 0.65):
        nz = random_smooth_noise(n, amp, rng)
        consider(a * np.exp(nz))
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def optimize_gradient_line(a, deadline, seed: int = 0, verbose: bool = False) -> tuple:
    a = normalize_positive(a, 1e-15)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 8:
        return a, internal_score(a)

    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = internal_score(a, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score <= 1.0000005:
        return best_a, best_score

    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    eta = 0.07
    tau_rel = 0.13
    focus_rel = 0.13
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    last_best = best_score

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c, fa, N = convolve_and_fft(a, N)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_score = float(score)
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_best = best_score

        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        focus = float(np.clip(focus_rel, 0.02, 0.35))
        tau = max(1e-12, float(tau_rel) * cmax)
        base = cmax - focus * cmax
        if base < 0.0:
            base = 0.0

        exact, smooth, cmax, c, w, g = score_grad_smooth(a, tau, base, N)
        if not np.isfinite(exact) or not np.isfinite(smooth):
            break

        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if scale <= 1e-15:
            stagnant += 1
            candidate = None
        else:
            g = g / scale
            eta_cands = []
            for mult in (2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                val = min(1.8, float(eta) * float(mult))
                if val >= 1e-6:
                    dup = any(abs(val - y) < 1e-5 for y in eta_cands)
                    if not dup:
                        eta_cands.append(val)
            eta_cands.sort(reverse=True)

            candidate = None
            best_key = None
            best_key_val = (2, float("inf"), float("inf"))

            for e in eta_cands:
                log_try = loga - e * g
                log_try -= float(np.mean(log_try))
                log_try = np.clip(log_try, -45.0, 0.0)
                a_try = np.exp(log_try)
                a_try /= float(a_try.sum())
                a_try = normalize_positive(a_try, 1e-15)
                c_try = convolve_seq(a_try, N)
                cmax_try = float(np.max(c_try))
                sc_try = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax_try
                if not np.isfinite(sc_try):
                    continue
                smooth_try = smooth_score_from_c(c_try, base, tau)
                if sc_try < score * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                    key = (0, sc_try, smooth_try)
                elif smooth_try < smooth * (1.0 - 1e-7) and sc_try < score * 1.025:
                    key = (1, sc_try, smooth_try)
                else:
                    key = None
                if key is not None and key < best_key_val:
                    best_key_val = key
                    best_key = key
                    candidate = (e, a_try, sc_try, smooth_try, log_try)

        accepted = candidate is not None
        if accepted:
            e, a_try, sc_try, smooth_try, log_try = candidate
            a = a_try
            loga = log_try
            loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
            score = float(sc_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_try)
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_best = best_score
            stagnant = 0
            eta = min(0.65, eta * 1.05)
            tau_rel = max(0.035, tau_rel * 0.9985)
            focus_rel = max(0.025, focus_rel * 0.9985)
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            eta = max(1e-4, eta * 0.72)
            if stagnant % 250 == 0:
                tau_rel = min(0.30, tau_rel * 1.35)
                focus_rel = min(0.32, focus_rel * 1.35)
                stagnant = 0

        if stagnant > 850:
            # Reheat from the current best plus a few smooth log-noise candidates.
            cand = None
            cand_score = best_score
            for amp in (0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.32, 0.55):
                nz = random_smooth_noise(n, amp, rng)
                log_try = np.log(best_a + 1e-300) + nz
                try_a = normalize_positive(np.exp(np.clip(log_try - np.max(log_try), -45.0, 0.0)), 1e-15)
                try_sc = internal_score(try_a, N)
                if np.isfinite(try_sc) and try_sc < cand_score:
                    cand_score = float(try_sc)
                    cand = try_a
            if cand is not None:
                a = cand.copy()
                loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
                loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
                if cand_score < best_score:
                    best_score = cand_score
                    best_a = a.copy()
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()
                loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
                loga -= float(np.mean(loga))
                a = best_a.copy()
            stagnant = 0
            eta = 0.09
            tau_rel = 0.16
            focus_rel = 0.16

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 400 == 0:
            print("gradline n={} iter={} best={:.9f}".format(n, it, best_score), flush=True)

        if best_score <= 1.001:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def optimize_adam(a, deadline, seed: int = 0) -> tuple:
    a = normalize_positive(a, 1e-15)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 8:
        return a, internal_score(a)

    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = internal_score(a, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score <= 1.0000005:
        return best_a, best_score

    x = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    x -= float(np.mean(x))
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = 0.035
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-12
    tau_rel = 0.12
    focus_rel = 0.12
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c, fa, N = convolve_and_fft(a, N)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_score = float(score)
            best_a = a.copy()

        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        base = cmax - float(focus_rel) * cmax
        tau = max(1e-12, float(tau_rel) * cmax)
        exact, smooth, cmax, c, w, gp = score_grad_smooth(a, tau, base, N)
        if not np.isfinite(exact):
            break

        dot = float(np.dot(a, gp))
        gx = a * (gp - dot)
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(gx)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-15:
            stagnant += 1
        else:
            gx /= scale
            t += 1
            m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gx
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gx * gx)
            mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
            vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
            step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
            np.clip(step, -0.35, 0.35, out=step)
            x_try = x - step
            x_try -= float(np.mean(x_try))
            x_try = np.clip(x_try, -45.0, 0.0)
            a_try = softmax_seq(x_try)
            a_try = normalize_positive(a_try, 1e-15)
            c_try = convolve_seq(a_try, N)
            cmax_try = float(np.max(c_try))
            sc_try = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax_try
            smooth_try = smooth_score_from_c(c_try, base, tau)

            accepted = False
            if np.isfinite(sc_try):
                if sc_try < score * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                    accepted = True
                elif smooth_try < smooth * (1.0 - 1e-7) and sc_try < score * 1.02:
                    accepted = True

            if accepted:
                x = x_try
                a = a_try
                score = float(sc_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc_try)
                    best_a = a.copy()
                stagnant = 0
                lr = min(0.12, lr * 1.04)
                tau_rel = max(0.035, tau_rel * 0.998)
                focus_rel = max(0.025, focus_rel * 0.998)
            else:
                stagnant += 1
                lr = max(1e-5, lr * 0.78)

        if stagnant > 900:
            # Reheat by pulling toward a smooth perturbation of the current best.
            cand = None
            cand_score = best_score
            for amp in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35):
                nz = random_smooth_noise(n, amp, rng)
                log_try = np.log(best_a + 1e-300) + nz
                try_a = normalize_positive(np.exp(np.clip(log_try - np.max(log_try), -45.0, 0.0)), 1e-15)
                try_sc = internal_score(try_a, N)
                if np.isfinite(try_sc) and try_sc < cand_score:
                    cand_score = float(try_sc)
                    cand = try_a
            if cand is not None:
                a = cand.copy()
                x = np.log(a + 1e-300)
                x -= float(np.mean(x))
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()
                x = np.log(a + 1e-300)
                x -= float(np.mean(x))
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = 0
            lr = 0.035
            stagnant = 0

        it += 1
        if best_score <= 1.001:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def choose_byPressure(vals, rng, pick_high: bool, temp: float = 0.15) -> int:
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(vals.size)
    if m == 0:
        return -1
    if m == 1:
        return 0
    vals = np.nan_to_num(vals, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    vmin = float(np.min(vals))
    vmax = float(np.max(vals))
    r = vmax - vmin
    if r <= 1e-14:
        return int(rng.integers(0, m))
    denom = max(float(temp), 1e-12) * r
    if pick_high:
        z = (vals - vmax) / denom
    else:
        z = (vmin - vals) / denom
    z = np.clip(z, -45.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return int(rng.integers(0, m))
    p /= s
    return int(rng.choice(m, p=p))


def make_binary_from_values(a, m: int) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(max(0, min(n, m)))
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= 0:
        return b
    if m >= n:
        b.fill(1.0)
        return b
    order = np.argsort(a, kind="stable")
    b[order[-m:]] = 1.0
    return b


def apply_binary_swap(b, c, two_b, i: int, j: int, n: int) -> None:
    # Assume b[i] == 1 and b[j] == 0.  This updates b, c, two_b in-place.
    c[i:i + n] -= two_b
    c[2 * i] += 1.0
    b[i] = 0.0
    two_b[i] = 0.0
    c[j:j + n] += two_b
    c[2 * j] += 1.0
    b[j] = 1.0
    two_b[j] = 2.0


def optimize_binary(a, deadline, seed: int = 0, m: int = None, target_score: float = None) -> tuple:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 16:
        return normalize_positive(a, 0.0), internal_score(a)

    if m is None:
        m = int(round(0.5 * n))
    m = int(max(2, min(n - 1, m)))

    b = make_binary_from_values(a, m)
    Nc = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c = np.rint(convolve_seq(b, Nc)).astype(np.float64)
    two_b = 2.0 * b

    best_b = b.copy()
    best_max = float(np.max(c))
    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_max / (float(m) * float(m))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    temp = 0.05
    beta = 0.12
    stale = 0
    it = 0

    Ngrad = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    target_max = None
    if target_score is not None and np.isfinite(target_score):
        target_max = max(1.0, float(target_score) * float(m) * float(m) / (2.0 * float(n)))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax / (float(m) * float(m))
        if score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_score = float(score)
            best_max = cmax
            best_b = b.copy()

        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break

        # Weights: always favor the current maximum, but if we are above an
        # integer target, mix strongly to the violating lags.
        base = cmax - beta * cmax
        tau = max(1e-12, beta * cmax)
        z = (c - base) / tau
        zmax = float(np.max(z))
        ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
        ss = float(ez.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            wtop = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            wtop = ez / ss

        if target_max is not None and cmax > target_max:
            base2 = max(1.0, 0.95 * target_max)
            tau2 = max(1.0, 0.20 * target_max)
            z2 = (c - base2) / tau2
            z2max = float(np.max(z2))
            ez2 = np.exp(z2 - z2max)
            ss2 = float(ez2.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ss2) or ss2 <= 0.0:
                wtarg = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                wtarg = ez2 / ss2
            w = 0.45 * wtop + 0.55 * wtarg
        else:
            w = wtop

        fa = np.fft.rfft(b, Ngrad)
        g = grad_from_weights(w, fa, Ngrad, n)

        one_idx = np.nonzero(b > 0.5)[0]
        zero_idx = np.nonzero(b < 0.5)[0]
        if one_idx.size == 0 or zero_idx.size == 0:
            break

        si = choose_byPressure(g[one_idx], rng, True, temp=0.18)
        sj = choose_byPressure(g[zero_idx], rng, False, temp=0.18)
        if si < 0 or sj < 0:
            break
        i = int(one_idx[si])
        j = int(zero_idx[sj])
        if i == j:
            continue

        old_score = score
        apply_binary_swap(b, c, two_b, i, j, n)
        new_max = float(np.max(c))
        new_score = 2.0 * float(n) * new_max / (float(m) * float(m))

        accept = False
        if new_score < old_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            accept = True
        elif new_score <= old_score * (1.0 + 1e-12):
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                accept = True
        else:
            delta = new_score - old_score
            if delta < 0.025 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / max(temp, 1e-12)):
                accept = True

        if accept:
            if new_score < best_score:
                best_score = float(new_score)
                best_max = new_max
                best_b = b.copy()
            stale = 0
            temp = max(0.0012, temp * 0.9985)
        else:
            # Correct inverse swap.
            apply_binary_swap(b, c, two_b, j, i, n)
            stale += 1
            temp = max(0.0006, temp * 0.965)
            if stale % 180 == 0:
                temp = min(0.16, temp * 1.9)
                beta = min(0.40, beta * 1.45)
                stale = 0
            if stale > 1400:
                break

        it += 1
        if it % 2500 == 0:
            c = np.rint(c)
            ssum = float(c.sum())
            if abs(ssum - float(m) * float(m)) > max(12.0, 1e-3 * float(m) * float(m)):
                c = np.rint(convolve_seq(b, Nc)).astype(np.float64)
                two_b = 2.0 * b

    return normalize_positive(best_b, 0.0), best_score


def propose_candidate():
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + 2350.0

    best_a = make_uniform(1024)
    best_score = internal_score(best_a)
    print("initial uniform score = {:.9f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    given = get_given_sequence()
    if given is not None:
        given = normalize_positive(given, 0.0)
        sc = internal_score(given)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = given.copy()
            print("given sequence score = {:.9f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    # Profile sweep over several lengths.
    for n in (512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1750.0:
            break
        budget_cut = min(time.monotonic(), deadline - 1750.0)
        seqs = generate_initial_sequences(n, 17 + n, max_count=24)
        for a0 in seqs:
            if time.monotonic() > budget_cut:
                break
            sc = internal_score(a0)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_a = normalize_positive(a0, 0.0)
                print("profile n={} score={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

    # Transformation sweeps around the best sequence at selected lengths.
    for n in (1024, 2048, 4096, 8192):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1350.0:
            break
        allow = min(90.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 1350.0)
        if allow < 20.0:
            break
        a0 = resample_seq(best_a, n)
        a1, sc1 = try_transforms(a0, time.monotonic() + allow)
        if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc1)
            best_a = normalize_positive(a1, 0.0)
            print("transforms n={} score={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

    # Main smooth gradient-search phases.
    plans = (
        (1024, 170),
        (2048, 310),
        (4096, 360),
        (8192, 360),
    )
    for n, allow in plans:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 520.0:
            break
        allow = min(float(allow), deadline - time.monotonic() - 520.0)
        if allow < 30.0:
            break

        starts = [resample_seq(best_a, n)]
        # Add a couple of raw profile starts for diversity.
        extra = generate_initial_sequences(n, 900 + n, max_count=5)
        starts.extend(extra[:2])

        per = max(20.0, allow / float(max(1, len(starts))))
        for idx, a0 in enumerate(starts):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 500.0:
                break
            sub_dead = min(deadline - 500.0, time.monotonic() + per * (1.2 if idx == 0 else 1.0))
            a1, sc1 = optimize_gradient_line(a0, sub_dead, seed=4242 + n + 13 * idx, verbose=False)
            if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc1)
                best_a = normalize_positive(a1, 0.0)
                print("gradient_line n={} start={} score={:.9f}".format(n, idx, best_score), flush=True)

    # small Adam polishing after gradient-line phases.
    for n in (2048, 4096, 8192):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 320.0:
            break
        allow = min(80.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 320.0)
        if allow < 25.0:
            break
        a0 = resample_seq(best_a, n)
        a1, sc1 = optimize_adam(a0, time.monotonic() + allow, seed=777000 + n)
        if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc1)
            best_a = normalize_positive(a1, 0.0)
            print("adam n={} score={:.9f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

    # Binary local search.  Binary arrays often expose different local basins.
    binary_plans = (
        (2048, 260),
        (4096, 300),
        (8192, 260),
    )
    target_hint = min(1.5027, max(1.02, best_score * 0.998))
    for n, allow in binary_plans:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 140.0:
            break
        allow = min(float(allow), deadline - time.monotonic() - 140.0)
        if allow < 45.0:
            break

        a_n = resample_seq(best_a, n)
        starts_b = []
        for frac in (0.40, 0.50, 0.62):
            starts_b.append(make_binary_from_values(a_n, int(round(frac * n))))
        # A couple of raw profile thresholds.
        for a0 in generate_initial_sequences(n, 555 + n, max_count=3):
            starts_b.append(make_binary_from_values(a0, int(round(0.5 * n))))

        per = max(20.0, allow / float(max(1, len(starts_b))))
        for idx, b0 in enumerate(starts_b):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            sub_dead = min(deadline - 120.0, time.monotonic() + per)
            a1, sc1 = optimize_binary(b0, sub_dead, seed=888000 + 17 * n + 5 * idx, target_score=target_hint)
            if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc1)
                best_a = normalize_positive(a1, 0.0)
                print("binary n={} start={} score={:.9f}".format(n, idx, best_score), flush=True)

    # Final polish and transformations.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        a1, sc1 = optimize_gradient_line(best_a, deadline - 20.0, seed=10101010, verbose=False)
        if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc1)
            best_a = normalize_positive(a1, 0.0)
            print("final gradient score={:.9f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        a1, sc1 = try_transforms(best_a, deadline - 4.0)
        if a1 is not None and np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc1)
            best_a = normalize_positive(a1, 0.0)
            print("final transforms score={:.9f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    best_a = normalize_positive(best_a, 0.0)
    out_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        final_eval = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(final_eval):
        out_list = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024
        final_eval = 2.0

    print("final internal score = {:.12f}".format(best_score), flush=True)
    print("final evaluated score = {:.12f}, length = {}".format(final_eval, len(out_list)), flush=True)
    return out_list
```
